[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import * as React from \'npm:react@18.3.1\'\nimport {\n  Body, Button, Container, Head, Heading, Html, Preview, Section, Text,\n} from \'npm:@react-email/components@0.0.22\'\nimport type { TemplateEntry } from \'./registry.ts\'\n\nconst SITE_NAME = \'LSAT U\'\nconst APP_URL = \'https://lsatprep.study\'\n\ninterface BookingConfirmationProps {\n  // "student" = the confirmation the student receives; "admin" = the copy Joshua receives\n  recipientRole?: \'student\' | \'admin\'\n  studentName?: string\n  studentEmail?: string\n  whenText?: string        // e.g. "Saturday, July 19 · 10:00 AM (Eastern Time)"\n  durationMinutes?: number\n  meetUrl?: string\n  studentNote?: string     // admin copy only: comment the student left at booking\n  fileNames?: string[]     // admin copy only: files the student attached\n  cancelUrl?: string       // Single Session capability; student copy only\n  calendarPending?: boolean\n}\n\nconst BookingConfirmationEmail = ({\n  recipientRole = \'student\',\n  studentName,\n  studentEmail,\n  whenText = \'your upcoming session\',\n  durationMinutes = 60,\n  meetUrl,\n  studentNote = \'\',\n  fileNames = [],\n  cancelUrl,\n  calendarPending = false,\n}: BookingConfirmationProps) => {\n  const isAdmin = recipientRole === \'admin\'\n  const hasSubmission = isAdmin && (Boolean(studentNote) || fileNames.length > 0)\n  return (\n    <Html lang="en" dir="ltr">\n      <Head />\n      <Preview>\n        {isAdmin\n          ? `${studentName ?? \'A student\'} booked a session — ${whenText}`\n          : `Your session with Joshua is booked — ${whenText}`}\n      </Preview>\n      <Body style={main}>\n        <Container style={container}>\n          <Heading style={h1}>\n            {isAdmin\n              ? \'New session booked\'\n              : studentName ? `You\'re booked, ${studentName}` : "You\'re booked"}\n          </Heading>\n\n          <Text style={text}>\n            {isAdmin\n              ? `${studentName ?? \'A student\'}${studentEmail ? ` (${studentEmail})` : \'\'} just booked a 1:1 session with you.`\n              : `Your ${durationMinutes}-minute session with Joshua is confirmed.`}\n          </Text>\n\n          <Section style={card}>\n            <Text style={cardLabel}>WHEN</Text>\n            <Text style={cardTitle}>{whenText}</Text>\n            <Text style={cardMeta}>{durationMinutes} minutes</Text>\n          </Section>\n\n          {hasSubmission && (\n            <Section style={submissionCard}>\n              <Text style={cardLabel}>STUDENT INCLUDED</Text>\n              {studentNote && <Text style={noteText}>{studentNote}</Text>}\n              {fileNames.length > 0 && (\n                <>\n                  <Text style={fileHeader}>{fileNames.length} file{fileNames.length === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} attached</Text>\n                  {fileNames.map((n, i) => (\n                    <Text key={i} style={fileItem}>📎 {n}</Text>\n                  ))}\n                  <Text style={fileHint}>Open the Bookings tab to download.</Text>\n                </>\n              )}\n            </Section>\n          )}\n\n          {!isAdmin && (\n            <Text style={text}>\n              {calendarPending\n                ? \'Your booking is confirmed. The calendar invite and Meet link will follow shortly.\'\n                : <>A Google Calendar invite is on its way to your email. <strong>Please accept it</strong> so\n                  the session lands on your calendar and you get reminders.</>}\n            </Text>\n          )}\n\n          {meetUrl && (\n            <Section style={{ textAlign: \'center\', margin: \'28px 0\' }}>\n              <Button href={meetUrl} style={button}>Join with Google Meet</Button>\n              <Text style={meetHint}>{meetUrl}</Text>\n            </Section>\n          )}\n\n          {!isAdmin && cancelUrl && (\n            <Text style={text}>\n              Need to cancel? <a href={cancelUrl}>Cancel this session</a>. Cancellations do not trigger an automatic refund.\n            </Text>\n          )}\n\n          <Text style={footer}>— The {SITE_NAME} Team</Text>\n        </Container>\n      </Body>\n    </Html>\n  )\n}\n\nexport const template = {\n  component: BookingConfirmationEmail,\n  subject: (d: Record<string, any>) =>\n    d?.recipientRole === \'admin\'\n      ? `New session booked${d?.studentName ? ` — ${d.studentName}` : \'\'} (${d?.whenText ?? \'\'})`\n      : `Your session with Joshua is booked — ${d?.whenText ?? \'\'}`,\n  displayName: \'Booking confirmation\',\n  previewData: {\n    recipientRole: \'admin\',\n    studentName: \'Alex\',\n    studentEmail: \'alex@example.com\',\n    whenText: \'Saturday, July 19 · 10:00 AM (Eastern Time)\',\n    durationMinutes: 60,\n    meetUrl: \'https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij\',\n    studentNote: \'Here are the two sections I did this week. I struggled with the last LR set, timing ran out.\',\n    fileNames: [\'PT155-section3.pdf\', \'timing-notes.png\'],\n  },\n} satisfies TemplateEntry\n\nconst main = { backgroundColor: \'#ffffff\', fontFamily: \'Inter, Arial, sans-serif\' }\nconst container = { padding: \'32px 28px\', maxWidth: \'560px\' }\nconst h1 = { fontSize: \'22px\', fontWeight: \'bold\', color: \'#0F172A\', margin: \'0 0 16px\' }\nconst text = { fontSize: \'15px\', color: \'#334155\', lineHeight: \'1.6\', margin: \'0 0 16px\' }\nconst card = {\n  background: \'#FAF7F2\',\n  border: \'1px solid #E8DCC8\',\n  borderRadius: \'12px\',\n  padding: \'18px 20px\',\n  margin: \'12px 0\',\n}\nconst cardLabel = { fontSize: \'11px\', letterSpacing: \'0.08em\', color: \'#A98B5B\', margin: \'0 0 4px\', fontWeight: 600 }\nconst cardTitle = { fontSize: \'17px\', fontWeight: 700, color: \'#7C5A2A\', margin: \'0 0 2px\' }\nconst cardMeta = { fontSize: \'13px\', color: \'#94A3B8\', margin: 0 }\nconst button = {\n  background: \'#7C5A2A\',\n  color: \'#ffffff\',\n  padding: \'12px 24px\',\n  borderRadius: \'10px\',\n  textDecoration: \'none\',\n  fontSize: \'14px\',\n  fontWeight: 600,\n  display: \'inline-block\',\n}\nconst meetHint = { fontSize: \'12px\', color: \'#94A3B8\', margin: \'10px 0 0\', wordBreak: \'break-all\' as const }\nconst submissionCard = {\n  background: \'#F8FAFC\',\n  border: \'1px solid #E2E8F0\',\n  borderRadius: \'12px\',\n  padding: \'16px 18px\',\n  margin: \'12px 0\',\n}\nconst noteText = { fontSize: \'14px\', color: \'#334155\', lineHeight: \'1.6\', margin: \'4px 0 0\', whiteSpace: \'pre-wrap\' as const }\nconst fileHeader = { fontSize: \'13px\', fontWeight: 600, color: \'#475569\', margin: \'12px 0 6px\' }\nconst fileItem = { fontSize: \'13px\', color: \'#334155\', margin: \'2px 0\' }\nconst fileHint = { fontSize: \'12px\', color: \'#94A3B8\', margin: \'8px 0 0\' }\nconst footer = { fontSize: \'12px\', color: \'#94A3B8\', margin: \'32px 0 0\' }\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import * as React from \'npm:react@18.3.1\'\nimport { renderAsync } from \'npm:@react-email/components@0.0.22\'\nimport { EmailAPIError, sendLovableEmail } from \'npm:@lovable.dev/email-js@0.1.0\'\nimport { TEMPLATES } from \'./registry.ts\'\n\n// Server-only: reads LOVABLE_API_KEY. Import from edge functions only — never\n// expose sending to the browser.\n\n// Configuration baked in at scaffold time\nconst SITE_NAME = "LSAT Attorney Helper"\n// SENDER_DOMAIN is the verified sender subdomain FQDN (e.g., "notify.example.com").\n// It MUST match the subdomain delegated to Lovable\'s nameservers. NEVER use the root domain.\nconst SENDER_DOMAIN = "notify.lsatprep.study"\n// FROM_DOMAIN is the domain shown in the From: header (e.g., "example.com").\n// Can be the root domain when display_from_root is enabled — this is cosmetic only.\nconst FROM_DOMAIN = "lsatprep.study"\n\nexport type SendTemplateEmailResult =\n  | { sent: true }\n  | { sent: false; reason: \'recipient_suppressed\' }\n\nexport interface SendTemplateEmailOptions {\n  templateData?: Record<string, any>\n  /** Dedupes retries of the same logical send; defaults to a random UUID (no dedupe). */\n  idempotencyKey?: string\n  replyTo?: string\n}\n\n/**\n * Renders a registered template and sends it through Lovable\'s managed email\n * API. Suppression, retries, and rate limits are enforced by Lovable\n * server-side. A suppressed recipient is an expected outcome\n * ({ sent: false }); any other failure throws — EmailAPIError exposes\n * .code and .status for branching.\n */\nexport async function sendTemplateEmail(\n  templateName: string,\n  to: string,\n  options: SendTemplateEmailOptions = {}\n): Promise<SendTemplateEmailResult> {\n  const apiKey = [REDACTED]  if (!apiKey) {\n    throw new Error(\'LOVABLE_API_KEY is not configured\')\n  }\n\n  const template = TEMPLATES[templateName]\n  if (!template) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Template \'${templateName}\' not found. Available: ${Object.keys(TEMPLATES).join(\', \')}`\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Template-level `to` takes precedence — notification templates always\n  // send to their fixed address.\n  const recipient = template.to || to\n  if (!recipient) {\n    throw new Error(\'Recipient is required (the template defines no fixed recipient)\')\n  }\n\n  const templateData = options.templateData ?? {}\n  const element = React.createElement(template.component, templateData)\n  const html = await renderAsync(element)\n  const text = await renderAsync(element, { plainText: true })\n  const subject =\n    typeof template.subject === \'function\'\n      ? template.subject(templateData)\n      : template.subject\n\n  try {\n    await sendLovableEmail(\n      {\n        to: recipient,\n        from: `${SITE_NAME} <noreply@${FROM_DOMAIN}>`,\n        sender_domain: SENDER_DOMAIN,\n        subject,\n        html,\n        text,\n        purpose: \'transactional\',\n        label: templateName,\n        idempotency_key: options.idempotencyKey || crypto.randomUUID(),\n        reply_to: options.replyTo,\n      },\n      { apiKey, sendUrl: Deno.env.get(\'LOVABLE_SEND_URL\') }\n    )\n  } catch (error) {\n    if (error instanceof EmailAPIError && error.code === \'recipient_suppressed\') {\n      return { sent: false, reason: \'recipient_suppressed\' }\n    }\n    throw error\n  }\n\n  return { sent: true }\n}\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\n\r\nimport { deleteEvent, getEvent, patchEvent, meetUrlFromEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";\r\nimport { decryptEnvelope } from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";\r\nimport { createRefund, retrieveRefund } from "../_shared/stripe.ts";\r\nimport { sendAppEmail } from "../_shared/sendAppEmail.ts";\r\n\r\nconst WORKER_NAME = "single-session-outbox";\r\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 1024;\r\n\r\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\r\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\r\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function authorizeTicket(supabase: any, req: Request): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const ticket = req.headers.get("x-worker-ticket") || "";\r\n  if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(ticket)) return false;\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("consume_booking_worker_ticket", {\r\n    _token: ticket,\r\n    _worker: WORKER_NAME,\r\n  });\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    console.error("worker ticket check failed", { code: error.code });\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  return data === true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction authorize(req: Request): boolean {\r\n  const expected = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET") ?? "";\r\n  if (!expected) return false;\r\n  const supplied = req.headers.get("x-worker-secret") || "";\r\n  if (expected.length !== supplied.length) return false;\r\n  let difference = 0;\r\n  for (let index = 0; index < expected.length; index++) difference |= expected.charCodeAt(index) ^ supplied.charCodeAt(index);\r\n  return difference === 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function heartbeat(supabase: any, phase: "start" | "success" | "error", error?: string, metadata: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\r\n  const { error: heartbeatError } = await supabase.rpc("record_booking_worker_heartbeat", {\r\n    _worker_name: WORKER_NAME,\r\n    _phase: phase,\r\n    _error: error ?? null,\r\n    _metadata: metadata,\r\n  });\r\n  if (heartbeatError) throw heartbeatError;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction whenText(startAt: string): string {\r\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\r\n    timeZone: "America/Toronto",\r\n    weekday: "long",\r\n    month: "long",\r\n    day: "numeric",\r\n    hour: "numeric",\r\n    minute: "2-digit",\r\n    timeZoneName: "short",\r\n  }).format(new Date(startAt));\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function sendEmail(job: any, booking: any, payload: any) {\r\n  const admin = job.recipient === "admin";\r\n  const recipientEmail = admin ? requiredEnv("BOOKING_ADMIN_EMAIL") : booking.email;\r\n  if (!recipientEmail) throw new Error("email_recipient_missing");\r\n  let templateName = "booking-confirmation";\r\n  let templateData: Record<string, unknown> = {\r\n    recipientRole: admin ? "admin" : "student",\r\n    studentName: booking.name,\r\n    studentEmail: booking.email,\r\n    whenText: whenText(booking.start_at),\r\n    durationMinutes: 70,\r\n    meetUrl: booking.meet_url,\r\n    studentNote: admin ? booking.notes : "",\r\n    fileNames: [],\r\n    cancelUrl: admin ? undefined : payload?.cancel_url,\r\n    calendarPending: !booking.meet_url,\r\n  };\r\n  if (["email_cancellation", "email_refund_apology"].includes(job.action)) {\r\n    templateName = "single-session-update";\r\n    templateData = {\r\n      kind: job.action === "email_refund_apology" ? "refund" : "cancellation",\r\n      recipientRole: admin ? "admin" : "booker",\r\n      studentName: booking.name,\r\n      whenText: whenText(booking.start_at),\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n  await sendAppEmail(templateName, recipientEmail, {\r\n    idempotencyKey: job.idempotency_key,\r\n    templateData,\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function complete(supabase: any, job: any, options: { providerStatus?: string; result?: any; meetUrl?: string } = {}) {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("complete_single_session_outbox", {\r\n    _job_id: job.id,\r\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\r\n    _provider_status: options.providerStatus ?? null,\r\n    _result: options.result ?? {},\r\n    _meet_url: options.meetUrl ?? null,\r\n  });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function fail(supabase: any, job: any, error: unknown, providerStatus?: string, confirmedFailure = false) {\r\n  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n  const { error: failureError } = await supabase.rpc("fail_single_session_outbox", {\r\n    _job_id: job.id,\r\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\r\n    _error: message,\r\n    _provider_status: providerStatus ?? null,\r\n    _confirmed_failure: confirmedFailure,\r\n  });\r\n  if (failureError && failureError.code !== "P0409") throw failureError;\r\n  return message;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\r\n  try {\r\n    const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\r\n    if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);\r\n    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n    // Two accepted callers: a worker holding BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET, and the\r\n    // scheduled job, which presents a single-use ticket only a service-role\r\n    // reader could have minted. No shared secret lives in SQL or cron metadata.\r\n    if (!authorize(req) && !(await authorizeTicket(supabase, req))) {\r\n      return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);\r\n    }\r\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "start");\r\n    const workerId = `${WORKER_NAME}:${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\r\n    const { data: jobs, error: claimError } = await supabase.rpc("claim_single_session_outbox", {\r\n      _worker_id: workerId,\r\n      _actions: null,\r\n      _limit: 20,\r\n      _lease_seconds: 180,\r\n    });\r\n    if (claimError) throw claimError;\r\n\r\n    const results: { id: string; action: string; outcome: string }[] = [];\r\n    for (const job of jobs ?? []) {\r\n      const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("*").eq("id", job.booking_id).maybeSingle();\r\n      if (bookingError || !booking) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, bookingError ?? new Error("booking_not_found"));\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n      if (booking.status !== job.precondition_status || booking.lifecycle_version !== job.precondition_lifecycle_version) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, new Error("lifecycle_precondition_changed"));\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "superseded" });\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      try {\r\n        if (job.action === "gcal_promote") {\r\n          if (!booking.gcal_event_id) throw new Error("tentative_event_missing");\r\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n          const targetCalendar = state?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n          const attendees = [booking.email, ...((booking.guests ?? []) as string[])].filter(Boolean).map((email) => ({ email }));\r\n          let event = await patchEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id, {\r\n            summary: `${String(booking.name || "Student").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0]} session`,\r\n            description: booking.notes ? `Booked via LSAT U.\\n\\nNotes from Booker:\\n${booking.notes}` : "Booked via LSAT U.",\r\n            attendees,\r\n            visibility: "default",\r\n            conferenceData: { createRequest: { requestId: booking.id, conferenceSolutionKey: { type: "hangoutsMeet" } } },\r\n            extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_single_session_booking_id: booking.id, lsatu_hold: "false" } },\r\n          }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n          let meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\r\n          if (!meetUrl) {\r\n            event = await getEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id);\r\n            meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\r\n          }\r\n          if (!meetUrl) throw new Error("meet_url_pending");\r\n          const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({\r\n            google_event_id: event.id,\r\n            calendar_id: targetCalendar,\r\n            title: event.summary,\r\n            description: event.description,\r\n            start_at: booking.start_at,\r\n            end_at: booking.end_at,\r\n            all_day: false,\r\n            status: event.status ?? "confirmed",\r\n            html_link: event.htmlLink ?? null,\r\n            meet_url: meetUrl,\r\n            source: "single_session",\r\n            etag: event.etag ?? null,\r\n            last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n            deleted_at: null,\r\n          }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" });\r\n          if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n          const completion = await complete(supabase, job, { meetUrl, result: { google_event_id: event.id } });\r\n          if (completion?.compensate) {\r\n            await deleteEvent(targetCalendar, event.id, { sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n            await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({ status: "cancelled", deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("google_event_id", event.id);\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (job.action === "gcal_delete") {\r\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n          const eventId = booking.gcal_event_id;\r\n          if (eventId) {\r\n            await deleteEvent(state?.calendar_id || "primary", eventId, { sendUpdates: booking.status === "cancelled" ? "all" : "none" });\r\n            const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({\r\n              status: "cancelled",\r\n              deleted_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n              last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n            }).eq("google_event_id", eventId);\r\n            if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n          }\r\n          await complete(supabase, job, { result: { google_event_id: eventId ?? null } });\r\n        } else if (job.action === "stripe_refund") {\r\n          if (!booking.stripe_payment_intent_id) throw new Error("payment_intent_missing");\r\n          const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n          const refund = booking.stripe_refund_id\r\n            ? await retrieveRefund(stripeSecret, booking.stripe_refund_id)\r\n            : await createRefund(stripeSecret, {\r\n              payment_intent: booking.stripe_payment_intent_id,\r\n              amount: 4500,\r\n              metadata: { booking_id: booking.id, product: "single_session" },\r\n            }, job.idempotency_key);\r\n          if (refund.status === "succeeded") {\r\n            await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: refund.status, result: { refund_id: refund.id } });\r\n          } else if (["failed", "canceled"].includes(String(refund.status))) {\r\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_confirmed_failed"), String(refund.status), true);\r\n          } else {\r\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_pending"), String(refund.status), false);\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (job.action.startsWith("email_")) {\r\n          const payload = job.payload_ciphertext\r\n            ? await decryptEnvelope(job.payload_ciphertext, requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY"))\r\n            : {};\r\n          await sendEmail(job, booking, payload);\r\n          await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: "accepted" });\r\n        } else {\r\n          throw new Error("unknown_outbox_action");\r\n        }\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "processed" });\r\n      } catch (error) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, error);\r\n        console.error("single-session outbox job failed", { bookingId: booking.id, jobId: job.id, action: job.action, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "success", undefined, { processed: results.length });\r\n    return json({ processed: results.length, results });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n    try {\r\n      const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n      await heartbeat(supabase, "error", message);\r\n    } catch (_) { /* fail-closed response is the durable alert when heartbeat is unavailable */ }\r\n    console.error("single-session-outbox error", { error: message });\r\n    return json({ error: "worker_failed" }, 500);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n'}]

You can also declare tickets.\n\n## Use cases\n\nFollow these examples to ensure that your `EventReservation` markup is properly structured.\n\nNote: Before you start, review how to embed schemas in emails and test schemas.\n\n### Basic event reminder without a ticket\n\nIf you are sending out an email confirming a user\'s attendance at an event, include the following markup. "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EventReservation",\n      "reservationNumber": "E123456789",\n      "reservationStatus": "http://schema.org/Confirmed",\n      "underName": {\n        "@type": "Person",\n        "name": "John Smith"\n      },\n      "reservationFor": {\n        "@type": "Event",\n        "name": "Foo Fighters Concert",\n        "startDate": "2027-03-06T19:30:00-08:00",\n        "location": {\n          "@type": "Place",\n          "name": "AT&T Park",\n          "address": {\n            "@type": "PostalAddress",\n            "streetAddress": "24 Willie Mays Plaza",\n seating, include `ticketToken`, `ticketNumber`, `venueSeat`, `venueRow`, and `venueSection`. You only need to include whichever of `venueSeat`, `venueRow`, and `venueSection` is needed to describe the location of the seat.\n\nDo not include `numSeats`.\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EventReservation",\n      "reservationNumber": "E123456789",\n      "reservationStatus": Fighters Concert",\n        "performer": {\n          "@type": "Organization",\n          "name": "The Foo Fighters",\n          "image": itemtype="http://schema.org/Event">\n        <meta itemprop="name" content="Foo Fighters Concert"/>\n### Multiple tickets\n\nThere are two options for handling multiple tickets:\n\n  * The first is to change `numSeats`. This means a single reservation represents tickets for `numSeats` number of individuals.\n\n  * If you want to have one ticket per attendee and include each attendee\'s name on the ticket, create multiple `EventReservation` items with `numSeats` set to 1. sender quality guidelines\n\n# Register with Google Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nYou can start testing schemas in email today. All schemas that you send to yourself (from `username@gmail.com` to `username@gmail.com`) are displayed in Google products.\n\nWhen you\'re ready to launch marked-up emails to your users, register with Google:\n\n  * Make sure that you meet all the guidelines and requirements listed below.\n  * Send an email from your production servers (or a server with similar `DKIM`, `SPF`, `From:`, and `Return-Path:` headers) including the markup or schema to `schema.whitelisting+sample@gmail.com`. Google checks that you comply with all the guidelines and requirements listed in registration guidelines.\n    * If you send a test or blank email, an email that doesn\'t contain a schema, or if you don\'t send an email for review, your application is discarded.\n    * Make sure that the markup is correct before sending the email. For more details, see Test your schemas. Ensure the email passes the Email Markup Tester without errors, and include as much data as possible.\n    * Gmail removes all markup when forwarding an email. Don\'t forward the email; send it directly.\n  * Fill out the registration form.\n\n## Registration guidelines\n\nTo process the schemas that you send, you must meet the following guidelines:\n\n### Email sender quality guidelines\n\n  * Emails must be authenticated using DKIM or SPF.\n  * The top-level domain (TLD) of the SPF check or DKIM signature must match the TLD of your `From:` email address.\n    * For example, if you use `From: user@example.com`, the DKIM or SPF must be for `example.com` or `sub.example.com`.\n    * Gmail only checks the SPF on the domain of your `Return-Path:` email. If you only rely on SPF and not DKIM, your `Return-Path:` email must match the TLD of your `From:` email.\n    * To quickly check if your SPF or DKIM settings are correct, see Email authentication.\n  * Emails must come from a static email address, such as `user@example.com`.\n  * Emails must follow the Prevent mail to Gmail users from being blocked or sent to spam guidelines.\n  * Consistent history of sending a high volume of mail from your domain (order of hundreds of emails a day minimum to Gmail) for at least a few weeks.\n  * A very low rate of spam complaints from users.\n\n### Actions and schema guidelines\n\n  * The highest-fidelity action available should be used. For example, if an interaction can be achieved by an in-app action (one-click, RSVP, review), that must be used. For more complex interactions, go-to actions can be used.\n  * Actions should be used for transactional mail where a high interaction rate is expected. They shouldn\'t be used on promotional bulk mail.\n  * Go-to actions:\n    * Must deep link into the specific page on which the action can be performed.\n    * Label of button needs to reflect a clear action to be taken and must be true to the page the user is going to.\n    * Label of action shouldn\'t contain punctuation or all caps. Must be short and concise.\n    * If an in-app action can be used for your use case, you must use the in-app action and not the go-to action (for example, "Verify Email Address" or "Review" should use in-app actions).\n    * Go-to actions are approved only for specific high-value use cases with high interaction rates (such as flight check-in and shipment tracking links).\n  * Low failure rate and fast response for services handling action requests.--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nJSON-LD \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/formats/json-ld?authuser=19)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn7search2\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: last month; Crawled: last month; Gmail supports JSON-LD data embedded in HTML documents with the `@context` of `schema.org`, as in the following example:    `<script type="application/ld+json"> ... The full specifications and requirements for the JSON-LD syntax are available on json-ld.org, and you can also use our Schema Validator tool to try out JSON-LD and debug your markup.\n\n# JSON-LD Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nJSON-LD is an easy-to-use JSON-based linked data format that defines the concept of `context` to specify the vocabulary for types and properties. Gmail supports JSON-LD data embedded in HTML documents with the `@context` of `schema.org`, as in the following example:\n    \n    `<script type="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "Person",\n      "name": "John Doe",\n      "jobTitle": "Graduate research assistant",\n      "affiliation": "University of Dreams",\n      "additionalName": "Johnny",\n      "url": "http://www.example.com",\n      "address": {\n        "@type": "PostalAddress",\n        "streetAddress": "1234 Peach Drive",\n        "addressLocality": "Wonderland",\n        "addressRegion": "Georgia"\n      }\n    }\n    </script>\n    `\n\nNote: the `@type` key is a reserved key name and should contain either a full type URI or a URI fragment (in which case a `http://schema.org/` prefix, derived from the supplied data-context attribute, is assumed).\n\nThe full specifications and requirements for the JSON-LD syntax are available on json-ld.org, and you can also use our Schema Validator tool to try out JSON-LD and debug your markup.\n\n\nExcept as otherwise noted, the content of this page is licensed under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 License, and code samples are licensed under the Apache 2.0 License. Gmail supports both JSON-LD and Microdata. You can use either format to mark up information in an email. This lets Google understand the fields and provide users with relevant search results, actions, and cards. For example, if the email is about an event reservation, you can annotate the start time, venue, number of tickets, and any other details that define the reservation.\n\n## Your first markup\n\nSuppose you\'re responsible for sending Google I/O tickets to participants and you want to mark up semantic information in these emails. At a minimum, your ticket confirmation email contains HTML similar to the following:\n    \n    `<html>\n      <body>\n        <p>\n          Dear John, thanks for booking your Google I/O ticket with us.\n        </p>\n        <p>\n          BOOKING DETAILS<br/>\n          Order for: John Smith<br/>\n          Event: Google I/O 2013<br/>\n          When: May 15th 2013 8:30am PST<br/>\n          Venue: Moscone Center, 800 Howard St., San Francisco, CA 94103<br/>\n          Reservation number: IO12345<br/>\n        </p>\n      </body>\n    </html>\n    `\n\nYou can add structured information anywhere inside the `<body>` element of the email HTML in any of the supported formats. The following code blocks show what the marked-up email looks like:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<h t ml>\n      <body>\n        <scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n        {\n          "@context":              "https://schema.org",\n          "@type":                 "EventReservation",\n          "reservationNumber":     "IO12345",\n          "underName": {\n            "@type":               "Person",\n            "name":                "John Smith"\n          },\n          "reservationFor": {\n            "@type":               "Event",\n            "name":                "Google I/O 2013",\n            "startDate":           "2013-05-15T08:30:00-08:00",\n            "location": {\n              "@type":             "Place",\n--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nEventReservation \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/types/EventReservation)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn7search4\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: 1.5 years ago; Crawled: 1.5 years ago; price  | Text  | Total price of the EventReservation. ... underName.email  | Text  | Email address.\n\n# EventReservation Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nType name: EventReservation\n\nExtends Reservation\n\nName  | Type  | Description\n--- | --- | ---\naction  | Action  | An action that can be taken upon this thing.\naction.name  | Text  | The string shown to the user on the UI element tied to the action.\naction.url  | URL  | Target url for the action. Use these examples to ensure that your markup is properly structured.\n\nNote: Before you start, make sure you understand how to embed schemas in emails and you are familiar with testing schemas.\n\n### Basic restaurant reservation\n\nThis is an example of the minimal amount of markup that will qualify your email as a `FoodEstablishmentReservation`.\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "FoodEstablishmentReservation",\n      "reservationNumber": "OT12345",\n      "reservationStatus": "http://schema.org/Confirmed",\n      "underName": {\n        "@type": "Person",\n        "name": "John Smith"\n--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSecure actions \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/actions/securing-actions?authuser=14)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn7search7\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: 4 days ago;   * Registration: The sender must register with Google.  * SPF or DKIM: Emails with schema markup must arrive from SPF- or DKIM-authenticated domains. ... There are various email forwarding and access patterns that Gmail handles in order to secure actions in emails.\n\n# Secure actions Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nThis page documents how Gmail secures the delivery and execution of actions.\n\n## Security measures enforced by Google\n\nThe following conditions must hold for schemas embedded in email:\n\n  * Registration: The sender must register with Google.\n  * SPF or DKIM: Emails with schema markup must arrive from SPF- or DKIM-authenticated domains.\n\n## Additional measures required for inline actions\n\nExtra security measures are required or recommended to secure inline actions:\n\n  * HTTPS: All actions must be handled using HTTPS URLs. Hosts must have valid SSL server certificates installed.\n  * Access Tokens: We recommend that senders using actions embed limited-use access tokens in action URLs to protect themselves against replay attacks. This is a recommended practice for any URL embedded in web pages or emails that might have side effects when invoked.\n--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nRegister with Google to send dynamic emails \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/ampemail/register?authuser=3&hl=en)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn7search8\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: last week; Google uses this email to verify compliance with all the guidelines and requirements. ... Your participation as a sender of dynamic email is subject to compliance with the Prevent mail to Gmail users from being blocked or sent to spam guidelines, which are subject to change without notice. ... If you want to register more than one sender email address, each address needs to be registered separately and needs to send its own production-quality email.\nGoogle can revoke your participation at any time if abuse or policy violations are discovered, or at Google\'s discretion. Google might request additional information during the registration process or after registration is complete to verify compliance.\n\n## Registration guidelines\n\nYou must meet the following guidelines to register successfully with Google:\n\n  * You must send a real, production-quality example email, not a demo or "Hello World" example. Don\'t put placeholder strings like `{name}` and `{count}` or test strings like `[Test]`, `[Proof mail]` and `[Sample]` in your subject or body.\n  * Emails must have a similar HTML or text MIME part. There are many instances where this is shown instead, such as when the user has disabled dynamic email.\n  * The email must have no bugs.\n  * You must authenticate the emails using SPF, DKIM and DMARC as detailed in the Security requirements.\n  * Ensure that you have reviewed the AMP for Email best practices, and that your email conforms to the suggestions there.\n  * Registration works on a per-sender-email-address basis. ## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "/* Booking product theme: light Calendly-style base with a deep burgundy\n   holographic accent. Scoped under .booking-holo so it can wrap both the\n   admin workspace and the student-facing booking pages without touching\n   the rest of the app. Overrides the shadcn HSL tokens locally. */\n\n.booking-holo {\n  --background: 350 20% 99%;\n  --foreground: 345 30% 12%;\n  --card: 0 0% 100%;\n  --card-foreground: 345 30% 12%;\n  --popover: 0 0% 100%;\n  --popover-foreground: 345 30% 12%;\n  --primary: 347 70% 27%;\n  --primary-foreground: 0 0% 100%;\n  --secondary: 348 30% 96%;\n  --secondary-foreground: 347 55% 25%;\n  --muted: 347 20% 96%;\n  --muted-foreground: 345 12% 44%;\n  --accent: 348 45% 94%;\n  --accent-foreground: 347 60% 24%;\n  --destructive: 0 72% 45%;\n  --destructive-foreground: 0 0% 100%;\n  --border: 345 18% 90%;\n  --input: 345 18% 87%;\n  --ring: 347 70% 35%;\n  color: hsl(var(--foreground));\n  background-color: hsl(var(--background));\n}\n\n.holo-text {\n  color: hsl(var(--primary));\n}\n\n.holo-surface {\n  /* Static rich burgundy gradient — deliberately NOT animated. The shadcn\n     Button base applies `transition-all` and swaps background-color on hover,\n     which fights any background keyframe animation frame-by-frame and reads\n     as flicker. Buttons stay still; only the wordmark carries the sheen. */\n  background: linear-gradient(120deg, #4f0d1c, #6e1423 45%, #8e1d3a) !important;\n  color: #fff !important;\n}\n\n.holo-surface:hover { filter: brightness(1.12); }\n\n.holo-ring { box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px hsl(347 70% 35% / 0.35), 0 8px 30px hsl(347 70% 25% / 0.12); }\n\n/* Soft burgundy-tinted elevation, the Calendly modal-shadow trick re-tinted. */\n.booking-shadow { box-shadow: 0 24px 72px hsl(345 60% 20% / 0.09), 0 0 20px hsl(345 60% 20% / 0.06); }\n\n@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {\n  .holo-surface { animation: none; }\n  .single-session-page *,\n  .single-session-page *::before,\n  .single-session-page *::after {\n    scroll-behavior: auto !important;\n    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;\n    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;\n    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;\n  }\n}\n\n/* The booking theme is deliberately light, which is right on the public\n   booking page a prospect lands on cold. Inside the dark classroom shell it\n   rendered as a 688x647 pure-white slab in a black page — the most jarring\n   thing a student meets on their first click into dark mode.\n\n   This re-voices the same burgundy palette dark, and is scoped to the\n   classroom so /book/* stays light for prospects. It cannot live in the app's\n   `.dark` class: that class is a deliberate alias that mirrors `.light`\n   (see index.css), so every shadcn token in the app stays light by design and\n   the classroom draws its own dark theme separately. */\nhtml.dark .classroom-root .booking-holo {\n  color-scheme: dark;\n  --background: 345 12% 9%;\n  --foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --card: 345 12% 13%;\n  --card-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --popover: 345 12% 13%;\n  --popover-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  /* Lightness is set by contrast, not by matching the light theme's 27%: the\n     accent is used as text on the card, and 58% measured 4.13:1 against it. */\n  --primary: 347 68% 68%;\n  --primary-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --secondary: 345 12% 19%;\n  --secondary-foreground: 345 15% 93%;\n  --muted: 345 10% 18%;\n  --muted-foreground: 345 10% 70%;\n  --accent: 345 15% 23%;\n  --accent-foreground: 345 15% 94%;\n  --destructive: 0 65% 58%;\n  --destructive-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --border: 345 10% 25%;\n  --input: 345 10% 27%;\n  --ring: 347 62% 52%;\n}\n\n/* DialogContent is portaled outside `.classroom-root`, so carry the same\n   classroom-dark tokens on the booking dialog itself. Public booking remains\n   light because only the dialog opts into this selector. */\nhtml.dark .booking-dialog.booking-holo {\n  color-scheme: dark;\n  --background: 345 12% 9%;\n  --foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --card: 345 12% 13%;\n  --card-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --popover: 345 12% 13%;\n  --popover-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --primary: 347 68% 68%;\n  --primary-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --secondary: 345 12% 19%;\n  --secondary-foreground: 345 15% 93%;\n  --muted: 345 10% 18%;\n  --muted-foreground: 345 10% 70%;\n  --accent: 345 15% 23%;\n  --accent-foreground: 345 15% 94%;\n  --destructive: 0 65% 58%;\n  --destructive-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --border: 345 10% 25%;\n  --input: 345 10% 27%;\n  --ring: 347 62% 52%;\n}\n\r\n"}]

outbox.\r\n-- The public edge function is the only public interface; both tables are service-role only.\r\n\r\nCREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_bookings (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  start_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  name text,\r\n  email text,\r\n  notes text,\r\n  guests jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,\r\n  client_idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  draft_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  checkout_request_fingerprint text,\r\n  checkout_fingerprint_disposition text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',\r\n  stripe_checkout_session_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_checkout_url text,\r\n  stripe_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_payment_intent_id text,\r\n  amount_cents integer,\r\n  currency text,\r\n  livemode boolean,\r\n  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\r\n  booking_token_hash text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  booking_token_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  cancel_token_hash text UNIQUE,\r\n  cancel_token_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_refund_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_refund_status text,\r\n  gcal_event_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  meet_url text,\r\n  lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',\r\n  status_changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  terminal_reason text,\r\n  ip_hash text,\r\n  sweep_worker_id text,\r\n  sweep_lease_token uuid,\r\n  sweep_lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  sweep_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_duration_chk CHECK (end_at = start_at + interval '70 minutes'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_guests_chk CHECK (jsonb_typeof(guests) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(guests) <= 10),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_lifecycle_version_chk CHECK (lifecycle_version >= 0),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_chk CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents = 4500),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_currency_chk CHECK (currency IS NULL OR lower(currency) = 'usd'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_status_chk CHECK (status IN (\r\n    'draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked', 'cancelled', 'expired', 'abandoned',\r\n    'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_fingerprint_disposition_chk CHECK (checkout_fingerprint_disposition IN (\r\n    'none', 'persisted', 'attached', 'stripe_expired', 'stripe_absent'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_checkout_expiry_chk CHECK (\r\n    (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NOT NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NOT NULL)\r\n  ),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_cancel_token_chk CHECK (\r\n    (cancel_token_hash IS NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (cancel_token_hash IS NOT NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at = start_at)\r\n  )\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_no_occupying_overlap\r\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (tstzrange(start_at, end_at, '[)') WITH &&)\r\n  WHERE (status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked'));\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_start_at_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (start_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_status_expiry_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (status, stripe_expires_at, draft_expires_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_email_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (lower(email)) WHERE email IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_user_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (user_id, start_at) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_sweep_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (sweep_lease_until, status, updated_at);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_outbox (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  booking_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.single_session_bookings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  action text NOT NULL,\r\n  recipient text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',\r\n  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',\r\n  attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  max_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 8,\r\n  lease_worker_id text,\r\n  lease_token uuid,\r\n  lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  next_retry_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  provider_status text,\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- What a bookable event IS, as data instead of as constants.\n--\n-- The single session\'s shape -- 70 minutes, 24 hours\' notice, no buffers, a six\n-- business day window, $45 -- is currently written into the edge function AND\n-- into three SQL functions that enforce the slot policy inside the booking\n-- transaction. That is why there can only ever be one kind of session: a\n-- consultation is 30 minutes with 30-minute buffers and a five day window, and\n-- there is nowhere to say so.\n--\n-- This migration only creates the vocabulary and seeds it with the values that\n-- are live today. Nothing reads it yet, so this changes no behavior. The SQL\n-- policy functions are switched over to it next, one at a time.\n--\n-- Every number here was read from Joshua\'s Cal.com account on 2026-08-30, which\n-- stays the reference for what these events are supposed to be.\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_event_types (\n  slug text PRIMARY KEY,\n  label text NOT NULL,\n  length_minutes int NOT NULL CHECK (length_minutes > 0),\n  -- Null means "start a slot every length_minutes", which is what Cal.com does\n  -- when no explicit interval is set. The single session\'s back-to-back 70\n  -- minute chain (1:00, 2:10, 3:20) is this rule, not a quirk.\n  slot_interval_minutes int CHECK (slot_interval_minutes IS NULL OR slot_interval_minutes > 0),\n  minimum_notice_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440 CHECK (minimum_notice_minutes >= 0),\n  buffer_before_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_before_minutes >= 0),\n  buffer_after_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_after_minutes >= 0),\n  -- Business days size the window; days inside it are offered whenever the\n  -- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.\n  window_business_days int NOT NULL DEFAULT 6 CHECK (window_business_days > 0),\n  price_cents int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\n  currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'usd\',\n  requires_payment boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nINSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (\n  slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,\n  buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,\n  price_cents, currency, requires_payment\n) VALUES\n  (\'single-session\', \'LSAT Session\', 70, NULL, 1440, 0,  0,  6, 4500, \'usd\', true),\n  (\'consultation\',   \'Consultation\', 30, NULL, 1440, 30, 30, 5,    0, \'usd\', false)\nON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;\n\n-- One student, one price. Joshua already runs this by hand on Cal.com -- there\n-- are twelve event types on that account and most of them exist only because a\n-- particular person pays a particular rate. Keyed by email because the booker\n-- is not necessarily signed in when they book.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_price_overrides (\n  email text NOT NULL,\n  event_slug text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  price_cents int NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\n  note text,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  PRIMARY KEY (email, event_slug)\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_price_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\n-- Students never read these directly: the booking page gets everything it needs\n-- from the edge function, which runs as service_role.\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking event types"\n  ON public.booking_event_types FOR ALL TO authenticated\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking price overrides"\n  ON public.booking_price_overrides FOR ALL TO authenticated\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_event_types TO authenticated;\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_price_overrides TO authenticated;\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_event_types TO service_role;\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_price_overrides TO service_role;\n\n-- The price a given booker pays for a given event: their override, else the\n-- event\'s own price. One definition, so the checkout and the payment\n-- verification can never disagree about what was owed.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(_event_slug text, _email text)\nRETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    (SELECT o.price_cents FROM public.booking_price_overrides o\n      WHERE o.event_slug = _event_slug AND lower(o.email) = lower(COALESCE(_email, \'\'))),\n    (SELECT t.price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _event_slug)\n  );\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) TO service_role;\n\r\n'}]

Note: Some of the schemas used by Google are still going through the standardization process of schema.org, and therefore, may change in the future.\n# EventReservation\n\nType name: EventReservation\n\nExtends Reservation\n\nName  | Type  | Description\n--- | --- | ---\naction  | Action  | An action that can be taken upon this thing.\naction.name  | Text  | The string shown to the user on the UI element tied to the action.\naction.url  | URL  | Target url for the action. If no explicit handler field is provided the action handler is expanded into a WebActionHandler with this url as the WebActionHandler url.\nadditionalTicketText  | Text  | Additional information about the ticket.\nbookingAgent  | Organization or Person  | Booking agent or agency. Also accepts a string (e.g. "").\nbookingAgent.image  | URL  | URL of an image of the Organization.\nbookingAgent.name  | Text  | Name of the agent/service.\nbookingAgent.url  | URL  | Website of the agent/service.\nbookingTime  | DateTime  | Date the reservation was made.\ncancelReservationUrl  | URL  | Web page where reservation can be cancelled.\nreservationFor.description  | Text  | A short description of the Event.\nreservationFor.doorTime  | DateTime  | The time admission will commence.\nreservationFor.endDate  | DateTime  | The end date and time of the event.\nreservationFor.image  | URL  | URL of an image of the Event.\nreservationFor.location\n(Required)  | Place  | The event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.address\n(Required)  | PostalAddress  | Address of the the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.address.addressCountry\n(Required)  | Text or Country  | Country of the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.address.addressLocality\n(Required)  | Text  | Locality (e.g. city) of the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.address.addressRegion\n(Required)  | Text  | Region (e.g. State) of the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.address.postalCode\n(Required)  | Text  | Postal code of the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.address.streetAddress\n(Required)  | Text  | Street address of the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.location.name\n(Required)  | Text  | Name of the the event\'s location.\nreservationFor.name\n(Required)  | Text  | Event\'s name.\nreservationFor.performer  | Person or Organization  | (recommended for Confirmation Cards/Search Answers) The event\'s performer. Also accepts an array of objects.\nreservationFor.performer.image  | URL  | (recommended for Confirmation Cards/Search Answers) URL of an image of the Person.\nreservationFor.performer.name  | Text  | (recommended for Confirmation Cards/Search Answers) Name of the Person.\nreservationFor.performer.url  | URL  | URL of the Person.\nreservationFor.startDate\n(Required)  | DateTime  | The start date and time of the event.\nreservationFor.url  | URL  | URL of the Event.\nreservationNumber\n(Required)  | Text  | The number or id of the reservation.\nreservationStatus\n(Required)  | ReservationStatus  | Current status of the reservation.\n--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nPlace \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/types/Place?authuser=2)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn8search1\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: 1.5 years ago; Crawled: 1.5 years ago; globalLocationNumber  | Text  | The Global Location Number (GLN, sometimes also referred to as International Location Number or ILN) of the respective organization, person, or place.\n\n# Place Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nType name: Place\n\nExtends Thing\n\nName  | Type  | Description\n--- | --- | ---\naddress  | PostalAddress  | Physical address of the item.\naggregateRating  | AggregateRating  | The overall rating, based on a collection of reviews or ratings, of the item.\ncontainedIn  | Place  | The basic containment relation between places.\nevent  | Event  | The event information.\nevents  | Event  | Upcoming or past events associated with this place or organization.\nfaxNumber  | Text  | The fax number.\ngeo  | GeoCoordinates or GeoShape  | The geo coordinates of the place.\nglobalLocationNumber  | Text  | The Global Location Number (GLN, sometimes also referred to as International Location Number or ILN) of the respective organization, person, or place. The GLN is a 13-digit number used to identify parties and physical locations.\nhasMap  | Map or URL  | A URL to a map of the place.\ninteractionCount  | Text  | A count of a specific user interactions with this item—for example, `20 UserLikes`, `5 UserComments`, or `300 UserDownloads`. You can also declare tickets.\n\n## Use cases\n\nFollow these examples to ensure that your `EventReservation` markup is properly structured.\n\nNote: Before you start, review how to embed schemas in emails and test schemas.\n\n### Basic event reminder without a ticket\n\nIf you are sending out an email confirming a user\'s attendance at an event, include the following markup. Whether planning an event with friends or organizing a business trip, email often assists with the task. By adding Schema.org markup to emails sent to users, you make that information available across their Google experience and enable quick actions, such as checking in for a flight. Gmail, Google Calendar, Google Search, and the Google app all use this structured data.\n\nSchema.org is an open standard markup vocabulary managed as a collaboration between Google and other companies. Markup embedded using Schema.org can be used by any email product that supports the standard.\n\nWatch the video for examples of how email markup can enhance your application.\n\nNote: Some schemas that Google uses are still going through the standardization process of Schema.org and might change in the future. For more information, see Schema.org proposals.\n\n## Gmail actions\n\nGmail uses Schema.org markup to accelerate user actions and to highlight the most important information users need from an email.\n\nGmail supports the following types of actions and interactive cards:\n\n  * One-click action for actions that users can perform with a single click\n  * Go-to action for more complex interactions\n  * Flight interactive cards\n\n## Gmail highlights\n\nGmail uses Schema.org markup to highlight the most important information within an email and allows users to act on it.\n\n  * Bus highlight\n  * Car rental highlight\n  * Flight highlight\n  * Event highlight for events with tickets\n  * Order and parcel delivery highlights for shopping\n  * Hotel highlight for hotel reservations\n  * Invoice highlight\n  * Restaurant highlight for restaurant reservations\n  * Train highlight\n\n## Gmail Promotions tab\n--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nהזמנת מקום באירוע \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/event-reservation?hl=he)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn8search8\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: 4 months ago; pass.session_length_minutes, meetUrl,\r\n          // Only the admin copy surfaces the submission.\r\n          studentNote: recipientRole === "admin" ? studentNote : "",\r\n          fileNames: recipientRole === "admin" ? uploadedFileNames : [],\r\n        },\r\n      });\r\n    } catch (mailErr) {\r\n      const message = mailErr instanceof Error ? mailErr.message : String(mailErr);\r\n      console.error(`confirmation email to ${recipientRole} failed`, { redemptionId, error: message });\r\n      const { error: alertErr } = await supabase.from("booking_calendar_alerts").insert({\r\n        redemption_id: redemptionId, alert_type: "persistence_failure",\r\n        details: { category: "confirmation_email", recipient_role: recipientRole, error: message },\r\n      });\r\n      if (alertErr && alertErr.code !== "23505") console.error("confirmation email alert failed", alertErr);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n  if (studentEmail) await sendConfirmation(studentEmail, "student");\r\n  if (opts.adminEmail) await sendConfirmation(opts.adminEmail, "admin");\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    ok: true,\r\n    was_existing: false,\r\n    redemption: {\r\n      id: redemptionId, start_at: startAt.toISOString(), end_at: endAt.toISOString(),\r\n      google_event_id: gEvent.id, html_link: gEvent.htmlLink ?? null, meet_url: meetUrl,\r\n      status: "booked", submission_status: submissionStatus,\r\n    },\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n'}]

You can validate your markup using the Email Markup Tester Tool.\n\n# Go-to actions Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nGo-to actions take the user to your website where the action can be completed. Unlike one-click actions, go-to actions can be interacted with multiple times.\n\nImage: Go-to actions in Gmail\n\n## Use cases\n\nGo-to actions supported by Gmail are:\n\n  * ViewAction\n  * TrackAction\n\nMore actions might be supported in the future.\n\nNote: Before you start, review how to embed schemas in emails and test schemas.\n\n## View Action\n\nYou can add a `ViewAction` button to emails requiring users to go to your site to complete the action.\n\nThe following declaration adds a `ViewAction` button to an email:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EmailMessage",\n      "potentialAction": {\n        "@type": "ViewAction",\n        "url": "https://watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123",\n        "name": "Watch movie"\n      },\n      "description": "Watch the \'Avengers\' movie online"\n    }\n    </scrip t>\n    \n    `\n\n### Microdata\n    \n    `<div itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/EmailMessage">\n      <div itemprop="potentialAction" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/ViewAction">\n        <link itemprop="target" href="https://watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123"/>\n        <meta itemprop="name" content="Watch movie"/>\n      </div>\n      <meta itemprop="description" content="Watch the \'Avengers\' movie online"/>\n    </div>\n    \n    `\n\n### Publisher data\n\nYou can add details about the organization sending the email message by setting the `publisher` field:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EmailMessage",\n      "description": "Watch the \'Avengers\' movie online",\n      "potentialAction": {\n        "@type": "ViewAction",\n        "url": "https://watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123",\n        "name": "Watch movie"\n      },\n      "publisher": {\n\nAn action is automatically generated when you specify the `trackingUrl` property. To link directly to a mobile application, also include a `TrackAction` as shown in the following example:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "ParcelDelivery",\n      "deliveryAddress": {\n        "@type": "PostalAddress",\n        "streetAddress": "24 Willie Mays Plaza",\n        "addressLocality": "San Francisco",\n        "addressRegion": "CA",\n        "addressCountry": "US",\n        "postalCode": "94107"\n      },\n      "expectedArrivalUntil": "2013-03-12T12:00:00-08:00",\n      "carrier": {\n        "@type": "Organization",\n        "name": "FedEx"\n      },\n      "itemShipped": {\n        "@type": "Product",\n        "name": "iPod Mini"\n      },\n      "partOfOrder": {\n        "@type": "Order",\n        "orderNumber": "176057",\n        "merchant": {\n          "@type": "Organization",\n          "name": "Bob Dole"\n        }\n      },\n      "trackingUrl": "http://fedex.com/track/1234567890"\n        <meta itemprop="orderNumber" content="176057"/>\n        <div itemprop="merchant" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/Organization">\n          <meta itemprop="name" content="Bob Dole"/>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <link itemprop="trackingUrl" href="http://fedex.com/track/1234567890"/>\n      <div itemprop="potentialAction" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/TrackAction">\n        <link itemprop="target" href="http://fedex.com/track/1234567890"/>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    \n    `\n\n## Test your markup\n\nYou can validate your markup using the Email Markup Tester Tool. Paste in your markup code and click the Validate button to scan the content and receive a report on any errors present.\n\n## Specification\n\nFor a specification of the various go-to action types, see the documentation for the specific type ViewAction or TrackAction.\n\nNote: Some schemas that Google uses are still going through the standardization process of Schema.org and might change in the future. For more information, see Schema.org proposals.--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nDeclare actions \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/actions/declaring-actions)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn9search1\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: 4 days ago; For example, to give an `EmailMessage` entity a `ViewAction` go-to link, populate the email\'s `potentialAction` property, as in the following example:### JSON-LD ... You declare in-app actions like go-to actions, but include extra information that helps email clients (such as Gmail) handle the action inline. ... When the user clicks the button, Google sends an HTTP request to your service, recording the confirmation.\n\n# Declare actions Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences.\n\nIn schema.org, an action represents an activity that can be performed on a piece of structured data. Gmail supports multiple types of actions, and you can define each with similar structured data.\n\n## Go-to actions\n\nIf you add markup to your content with schema.org entities, you can add go-to actions for them. For example, to give an `EmailMessage` entity a `ViewAction` go-to link, populate the email\'s `potentialAction` property, as in the following example:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EmailMessage",\n      "potentialAction": {\n        "@type": "ViewAction",\n        "target": "https://watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123",\n        "name": "Watch movie"\n      },\n      "description": "Watch the \'Avengers\' movie online"\n    }\n    </scrip t>\n    \n    `\n\n### Microdata\n    \n    `<div itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/EmailMessage">\n      <div itemprop="potentialAction" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/ViewAction">\n        <link itemprop="target" href="https://watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123"/>\n        <meta itemprop="name" content="Watch movie"/>\n      </div>\n      <meta itemprop="description" content="Watch the \'Avengers\' movie online"/>\n    </div>\n    \n    `\n\nOther email clients that don\'t support schemas in emails automatically ignore this markup.\n\n## Mobile deep linking\n\nGo-to actions can also link directly to content in mobile apps on Android and iOS. To deep link to an app, include additional `target` URLs encoded with the `android-app://` and `ios-app://` schemes, as shown in the following examples:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `"target": [\n      "<web url>",\n      "android-app://<android package name>/<scheme>/<host>/<path+query>",\n      "ios-app://<App store ID>/<scheme>/<host><path+query>"\n    ]\n    `\n\n### Microdata\n    \n    `<link itemprop="target" href="<web url>"/>\n    <link itemprop="target" href="android-app://<android package name>/<scheme>/<host>/<path+query>"/>\n    <link itemprop="target" href="ios-app://<App store ID>/<scheme>/<host>/<path+query>"/>\n    `\n\nExtending the previous `EmailMessage` example:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EmailMessage",\n      "name": "Watch movie",\n      ... i nf orma t io n abou t t he movie ...\n      "potentialAction": {\n        "@type": "ViewAction",\n        "target": [\n          "https://watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123",\n          "android-app://com.watchmovies.app/http/watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123",\n          "ios-app://12345/movieapp/watch-movies.com/watch?movieId=abc123"\n        ]\n      }\n    }\n    </scrip t>\n    `\n\n### Microdata\n    \n    `<div itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/EmailMessage">\n      <meta itemprop="name" content="Watch movie"/>\n      ... information about the movie ...\n      <div itemprop="potentialAction" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/ViewAction">\nUsers can only interact with a `ConfirmAction` once.\n\nThe following example adds a `ConfirmAction` button to an email about an expense report:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EmailMessage",\n      "potentialAction": {\n        "@type": "ConfirmAction",\n        "name": "Approve Expense",\n        "handler": {\n          "@type": "HttpActionHandler",\n          "url": "https://myexpenses.com/approve?expenseId=abc123"\n        }\n      },\n      "description": "Approval request for John\'s $10.13 expense for office supplies"\n    }\n    </scrip t>\n    \n    `\n\n### Microdata\n    \n    `<div itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/EmailMessage">\n      <div itemprop="potentialAction" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/ConfirmAction">\n        <meta itemprop="name" content="Approve Expense"/>\n        <div itemprop="handler" itemscope itemtype="http://schema.org/HttpActionHandler">\n          <link itemprop="url" href="https://myexpenses.com/approve?expenseId=abc123"/>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <meta itemprop="description" content="Approval request for John\'s $10.13 expense for office supplies"/>\n    </div>\n    \n    `\n\n## Expiring actions\n\nIn many cases, actions are only relevant for a limited period of time. Actions associated with entities with known dates, like travel reservations, automatically expire. Gmail doesn\'t display the action after the trip has passed.\n\nYou can also explicitly add expirations to actions. For example, an action to clip a coupon or save an offer code might only be valid for a limited time. To set the time window for when an action is displayed, set the `startTime` and `endTime` properties of the action:\n\n### JSON-LD\n    \n    `<scrip t t ype="application/ld+json">\n    {\n      "@context": "http://schema.org",\n      "@type": "EmailMessage",\n      "potentialAction": {\n        "@type": "ConfirmAction",\n        "name": "Save coupon",\n        "handler":  {\n           "@type": "HttpActionHandler",\n           "url": "https://my-coupons.com/approve?--------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nViewAction \xa0|\xa0 Gmail \xa0|\xa0 Google for Developers (https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/types/ViewAction)\n\ue200cite\ue202turn9search2\ue201 [wordlim: 200] Published: 1.5 years ago; Crawled: 1.5 years ago; # ViewAction Stay organized with collections Save and categorize content based on your preferences. ... ## assistant

Email implementation mapping is complete and sent to the parent agent.

Key finding: React Email preserves JSON-LD and Lovable’s client forwards the HTML unchanged, but Lovable’s managed server and Gmail rendering still require a real-message verification. Gmail’s blue event panel cannot be recolored, and LSAT U may not yet meet Google’s sender-volume registration requirement.
